### [115] [Inferring radio beams from sparse sampling patterns: a case study with the Murchison Widefield Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00466)
*Michael J. Wilensky,Aman Chokshi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文利用重新处理的卫星观测数据，推断出默奇森宽场阵列（MWA）接收单元在137 MHz下的天顶指向功率波束，并通过两种贝叶斯方法（物理驱动的偶极子变异性模型与分层样条模型）量化其相对于电磁仿真的偏差，揭示了波束零点变浅偏移及旁瓣相干畸变等特征，验证了卫星信号可用于可重复、实用的波束重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高动态范围射电实验（尤其是宇宙再电离时期21-cm信号探测）亟需精确的主波束模型；波束误差会将强前景辐射耦合进微弱的宇宙学信号中，因此需实测校准。

Method: 基于重处理的卫星观测数据，构建两种互补贝叶斯模型：一是物理驱动的复数偶极子差异模型，二是用于灵活建模方向相关结构的分层样条模型。

Result: 观测揭示波束零点变浅且偏移、旁瓣出现相干畸变；偶极子模型能捕捉大部分大尺度结构，样条模型进一步恢复局域细节且对孤立伪影鲁棒；四个独立子数据集均一致重现可见半球内的零点与旁瓣形态。

Conclusion: 机会性卫星信号可支撑实用、可重复的波束重建；结果推动未来开展色散校正、相位参考的全琼斯响应测量。

Abstract: Accurate primary-beam models are essential for high-dynamic-range radio experiments, particularly searches for the redshifted 21-cm signal from the Epoch of Reionisation, where beam errors can couple bright foreground emission into the faint cosmological measurement. Using reprocessed satellite observations we infer the 137-MHz, zenith-pointed power beams of Murchison Widefield Array receiving elements. We develop two complementary Bayesian approaches for quantifying departures from nominal electromagnetic simulations: a physically motivated model of complex variations among individual dipoles and a hierarchical spline model for flexible direction-dependent structure. The measurements reveal shallower and displaced nulls together with coherent sidelobe deformations. The dipole model captures much of this broad structure, whereas the spline recovers additional localised features while remaining largely robust to isolated pass-shaped artefacts. Despite their sparse and distinct sampling patterns, four independent 20-day subsets, each containing approximately 20\% of the full dataset, recover consistent null and sidelobe morphology across the visible hemisphere. These results demonstrate that opportunistic satellite transmissions can support practical, repeatable beam reconstruction, while motivating future chromatic, phase-referenced measurements of the full Jones response.

</details>


### [116] [NASA ASTRA White Paper : A Chandra Successor in the 2030s](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00475)
*C. S. Reynolds,E. Hodges-Kluck,E. A. Kara,M. Koss,K. Madsen,E. Miller,B. Williams,S. Allen,M. Bautz,K. Burdge,N. Cappelluti,B. Cenko,L. Corrales,A. Falcone,A. Foord,D. Haggard,L. Lopez,R. Mushotzky,A. Ptak,H. Russell,S. Safi-Harb,K. Stassun*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项Chandra望远镜的后继X射线天文任务（AXIS系列），旨在提升灵敏度与巡天速度，以支撑多领域前沿天体物理研究，并为未来旗舰级X射线任务验证关键技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chandra虽成果卓著，但灵敏度和巡天速度已无法匹配JWST、Roman、LISA等新一代大型设施，亟需更高性能的X射线观测平台。

Method: 基于NASA Probe概念研究的AXIS（Advanced X-ray Imaging Satellite）框架，设计三类任务方案：AXIS-Deep（深场高灵敏）、AXIS-Classic（均衡型）、AXIS-Fast（高速巡天），并分析其科学目标与仪器权衡。

Result: 明确了四大高优先级科学问题——超大质量黑洞起源、星系中恒星/AGN反馈机制、高能恒星活动对系外行星大气的影响、黑洞并合过程；并论证了2030年代发射该任务对技术验证与人才延续的关键作用。

Conclusion: AXIS系列任务是衔接当前与未来X射线天文学的关键一步，兼具突破性科学能力与战略支撑价值。

Abstract: Chandra established that soft X-ray spectral imaging with approximately 1 arcsec angular resolution is an essential tool for almost every area of astrophysics, from the early Universe to ice chemistry in protoplanetary disks. While Chandra continues to deliver groundbreaking science, it lacks the sensitivity to be the counterpart to JWST, Roman, Rubin, LISA, ngVLA, ALMA, IceCube, NewAthena, ELT, and other upcoming large facilities. Here we highlight the scientific potential of a Chandra successor with >10x higher sensitivity and >50x survey speed. While the scientific reach of such a mission is very broad, we showcase four high-priority science questions that require these capabilities -- How did supermassive black holes form?, How does stellar and AGN feedback work in galaxies?, How does high-energy stellar activity affect exoplanet atmospheres? and, How do black holes merge? A Chandra successor in the 2030s would also demonstrate the X-ray mirror technology for a future flagship and sustain the US high energy astrophysics workforce until that flagship launches. We outline the science-instrument trade space by sketching three missions based on the NASA Probe concept study report for the Advanced X-ray Imaging Satellite (AXIS): AXIS-Deep, AXIS-Classic, and AXIS-Fast, highlighting a breakthrough science case for each and listing others that drive the design.

</details>


### [117] [Automated multi-stressor optical fiber focal ratio degradation and throughput characterization system for future massive multiplex spectroscopic survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00507)
*Daksh Singh,Ting S. Li,Sarik Jerma,Shaojie Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化多应力光纤表征平台，用于高效测量大数量光学纤维的焦比退化（FRD）和相对透过率，并系统研究其在不同光学与机械应力条件下的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来光谱巡天设备（如Spec-S5、WST）将配备超2.5万根光纤，手动表征其FRD和透过率耗时巨大，亟需自动化、高通量的表征方法。

Method: 构建了可同时调控输入角度、波长、弯曲半径、扭转角度及外部载荷等多应力参数的自动化光学平台；开发定制软件框架实现硬件控制、数据采集与分析全流程自动化。

Result: 验证实验表明该平台能分辨显著及细微的应力依赖性FRD与透过率变化；发现FRD明显依赖输入角与弯曲半径，对压缩载荷较不敏感，且呈现周期性扭转角依赖；透过率随输入角下降不能仅由投影面积模型解释。

Conclusion: 该自动化、可扩展平台为下一代大规模光谱巡天设施所需的光纤批量表征提供了通用、高效的技术支撑。

Abstract: Future spectroscopic survey facilities such as Spec-S5 and the Wide-Field Spectroscopic Telescope (WST) will contain more than 25,000 optical fibers. Characterizing the focal ratio degradation (FRD) and throughput of these fibers is essential for spectroscopic calibration, sky subtraction, and overall instrument performance, but manual testing of such large fiber populations is prohibitively time-consuming. In this work, we present an automated multi-stressor optical fiber characterization platform capable of measuring both FRD and relative throughput while systematically varying optical and mechanical stress conditions. The system enables characterization as a function of input angle, wavelength, bend radius, twist angle, and externally applied load, while also allowing combinations of stressors to be investigated simultaneously. A custom software framework automates hardware control, data acquisition, and data analysis, enabling large characterization campaigns to be performed with minimal user intervention. Validation measurements performed on a multimode optical fiber demonstrate the ability of the platform to resolve both pronounced and subtle stress-dependent variations in FRD and relative throughput. The results show the expected dependence of FRD on input angle and bend radius, reveal comparatively weak sensitivity to externally applied compressive loading under the investigated conditions, and identify a reproducible periodic dependence on fiber twist angle. In addition, relative throughput measurements indicate that the reduction in relative throughput with increasing input angle cannot be explained solely by a simple projected-area model. The automated workflow and scalability of the system provide a versatile framework for the large-scale characterization of optical fibers required by next-generation spectroscopic survey facilities.

</details>


### [118] [Prototyping the Next-Generation Spectrograph for Space Exploration: Digital Micromirror Device-based Multi-Object Spectrograph (DMD-MOS) Calibration and Performance Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00509)
*Jiayi Emma Xu,Sarik Jeram,Shaojie Chen,Ting S. Li,Ziang Chen,Zain Azam,Adi Khandelwal,Mark Barnet,Yixuan Cheng,Suresh Sivanandam*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于数字微镜器件（DMD）的多目标光谱仪（DMD-MOS），通过双通道设计（成像与光谱通道）实现目标选择与光谱观测同步进行，并在实验室中验证了其光谱分辨率、线展宽函数、波长覆盖及DMD与成像平面间的映射关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多目标光谱仪方法存在局限，需探索更鲁棒、灵活的 slit mask 生成方式；DMD 提供可编程、快速切换的微镜阵列，适合动态多目标观测。

Method: 设计并构建基于DMD的双通道光学系统：一个成像通道用于目标选择，一个光谱通道用于光谱获取；通过控制DMD微镜角度将选定目标的光导入光谱通道；开展实验室测试以表征关键性能参数。

Result: 成功实现DMD-MOS原型机的实验室验证，获得光谱分辨率、线展宽函数、波长覆盖范围等关键指标，并建立了DMD微镜位置与成像视场之间的精确映射关系，支持实时目标配置。

Conclusion: DMD-MOS是一种可行且具优势的新型多目标光谱观测技术，其双通道架构与DMD动态掩模能力为未来天文巡天设备提供了高灵活性与高效率的新路径。

Abstract: Abandoning the traditional approaches to multi-object spectrographs, the Digital Micro-mirror Device-based Multi-Object Spectrograph (DMD-MOS) leverages a robust, elegant method for creating multi-object spectroscopy slit masks through use of a DMD, which is an array of microscopic flat mirrors where each micro-mirror can be addressed to physically flip between two discrete angles. We have designed two optical channels for DMD-MOS - one for each of these two positions: 1) an imaging channel that can be used for multi-object target selection and 2) a spectrograph channel. Using this two-channel approach, DMD-MOS can simultaneously image the telescope field of view (FOV) with the imaging channel and actuate the specific DMD micro-mirrors to send light from chosen targets into the spectrograph channel. In this work, we present the results of our laboratory testing of DMD-MOS including spectral resolution, line spread function, wavelength coverage, and the mapping between the DMD and imaging channel planes, the latter of which enables DMD-MOS and our control software to instantaneously configure the DMD to observe chosen target(s) in the acquisition field.

</details>


### [119] [Measuring the linear gain performance for the OrcaQuest CMOS imaging sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00570)
*Zain Azama,Aditya Khandelwal,Ryan Dungee,Ting S. Li,Sarik Jeram,Shaojie Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本论文针对Hamamatsu Photonics公司的OrcaQuest CMOS传感器在短曝光时间下表现出的非线性增益曲线问题，通过多种曝光测试和数据处理流程进行了表征，并提供了更精确的增益校准曲线，以提升像素增益、线性度及高帧率图像测量的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OrcaQuest CMOS传感器在低曝光时间下存在非线性增益曲线，影响测量精度和图像一致性，需进行准确表征与校正。

Method: 通过多种曝光时间下的实验测试，并结合详细的数据还原处理流程，对传感器的增益特性进行系统表征。

Result: 获得了更准确的增益校准曲线，可用于改善像素增益估计、线性度评估及高帧率成像性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为OrcaQuest传感器在低曝光条件下的定量成像应用提供了关键校准依据，提升了其在精密光学测量中的可靠性。

Abstract: The OrcaQuest CMOS sensor by Hamamatsu Photonics has been found to exhibit a non-linear gain curve at low exposure times. This effect is characterized through the various exposure tests and data reduction pipelines detailed in this report, and a more accurate gain characterization curve is provided. Such data can be used to provide more consistent measures of pixel gain, linearity, and high framerate images.

</details>


### [120] [Near-Noiseless Single-Photon Detection in the Infrared with a Megapixel Semiconductor Array for Low-Background Astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00799)
*Guillaume Huber,Pavaman Bilgi,Charles-Antoine Claveau,Shane Jacobson,Ian Baker,Daniel Owton,Vincent Isgar,Chris Maxey,Markus Loose,Michael Bottom*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文展示了在常规低温下工作的百万像素HgCdTe线性模式雪崩光电二极管（LmAPD）阵列实现单光子分辨能力，通过雪崩增益将光子信号提升至读出噪声之上，同时抑制暗电流影响，为未来低背景红外天文观测提供了有前景的半导体探测器方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外波段单光子探测长期依赖需极低温运行的超导探测器，系统复杂、功耗高；而半导体方案如HgCdTe雪崩二极管受限于暗电流和隧穿效应，难以用于极微弱光场景。

Method: 采用非破坏性上坡读出（non-destructive up-the-ramp）方式采集数据，利用HgCdTe线性模式雪崩光电二极管（LmAPD）的雪崩增益放大光子诱导电荷信号，同时保持暗信号基本不被放大，并在常规低温（非近绝对零度）下验证其单光子分辨性能。

Result: 实现了百万像素级HgCdTe LmAPD阵列在常规低温下的单光子分辨操作，光子信号显著高于读出噪声，假阳性率约为每千次读数每像素2次。

Conclusion: HgCdTe LmAPD是一种可行且有前景的红外光子计数焦平面技术路径，有望替代超导探测器用于下一代低背景天文观测系统。

Abstract: Sensor noise is a fundamental barrier to low-flux astronomical imaging and spectroscopy, where the ideal sensor would unambiguously distinguish individual photon arrivals. While optical semiconductor arrays can reach single-photon sensitivity, comparable performance in the infrared has largely required superconducting detectors. These must be operated at temperatures near absolute zero and require substantially more power, introducing major system-level challenges. HgCdTe avalanche photodiodes offer a semiconductor alternative by amplifying charge before readout, but dark current and tunneling effects have limited their use in the faintest regimes.
  Here we demonstrate single-photon-resolving operation in a megapixel-format HgCdTe linear-mode avalanche photodiode array operated at conventional cryogenic temperature. Avalanche gain raises photon-induced steps in non-destructive up-the-ramp data above the readout-noise floor while leaving the dark signal largely unamplified, with a false positive rate of approximately 2 false counts per thousand reads per pixel. These results establish LmAPDs as a promising route toward infrared photon-counting focal planes for future low-background astronomical observatories.

</details>


### [121] [LeoNet: A Machine Learning Method for Binary Pulsar Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00908)
*Zhaocheng Gong,Jack White,Zeyu Yang,Jayanta Roy,Karel Adamek,Wesley Armour*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出LeoNet，一种用于二元脉冲星搜索的卷积神经网络，通过10个可学习滤波器提取受多普勒频移影响的信号特征，在检测性能和处理速度上显著优于传统PRESTO加速搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元脉冲星是检验引力理论的重要实验室，但轨道多普勒频移使探测复杂化；传统傅里叶域加速度/急动度搜索计算成本高，需更高效方法。

Method: 设计LeoNet卷积神经网络，使用10个可学习滤波器提取信号特征，生成10通道特征图替代显式采样的加速度-急动度响应网格，并由卷积分类器识别候选信号。

Result: 在500秒模拟观测中，相比PRESTO加速度搜索，LeoNet平均假阴率相对降低55.5%；在NVIDIA H100 GPU上处理单次观测仅需3.44–4.37毫秒，相较PRESTO FDAS在CPU上的1.767秒，提速约499倍。

Conclusion: LeoNet兼具更高检测性能与毫秒级处理能力，适合作为实时二元脉冲星搜索流水线中的候选信号识别模块。

Abstract: Binary pulsars provide valuable laboratories for testing theories of gravity, but orbital Doppler shifts complicate their detection. Fourier-domain acceleration and jerk searches address this challenge via matched filtering, but at substantial computational cost. We present LeoNet, a convolutional neural network that uses ten learnable filters to extract features of signals affected by Doppler shifts. The resulting ten-channel feature map provides a compact, lower-dimensional alternative to an explicitly sampled acceleration-jerk response grid and is analysed by a convolutional classifier to identify candidate signals. For simulated observations lasting 500 s, LeoNet achieves a mean relative reduction in false negative rate of 55.5% across five sampling intervals compared with the evaluated PRESTO acceleration-search configuration. TensorRT-optimised LeoNet processes each 500 s observation in 3.44-4.37 ms in FP32 on an NVIDIA H100 PCIe GPU across eight sampling intervals, including preprocessing, inference, and postprocessing. At a sampling interval of 128 microseconds, its mean processing time is 3.54 ms, compared with 1.767 s for PRESTO FDAS on an AMD EPYC 9825 CPU with search-frequency limits of 96-1000 Hz, corresponding to an approximately 499-fold speedup in the measured processing time. These results suggest that LeoNet has the potential to improve detection performance, while its millisecond-scale processing time supports its use as a candidate-identification stage in real-time binary pulsar search pipelines.

</details>


### [122] [Comparing Optimized Systematic Error Correction Methods on Selected TESS Light Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01040)
*David Rapetti,Jon Jenkins,Joseph Twicken,Douglas Caldwell,Jeffrey Smith*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SysCoCoPy软件包，用于评估和比较TESS光变曲线系统误差校正方法（包括SPOC的PDC及三种社区开发的校正器）的性能，特别关注地球与月球散射光污染的去除效果；结果表明，尽管两种社区校正器在自动参数优化下对散射光校正效果较好，但PDC整体上更稳健、成功率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TESS光变曲线中的系统误差（尤其是地球和月球散射光污染）严重影响天体物理分析，亟需客观、可扩展的方法来评估不同校正方法的性能。

Method: 开发了SysCoCoPy软件包，集成SPOC的PDC及三种基于Lightkurve实现的社区校正器，并构建自动参数优化框架以支持大规模样本分析；提供定性与定量对比工具，涵盖个案分析与统计评估。

Result: 自动优化显著提升了其中两种校正器对散射光的校正成功率；但在最大样本的统计分析中，PDC展现出更高的整体稳健性和指标成功率。

Conclusion: SysCoCoPy为TESS系统误差校正提供了标准化、可扩展的评估平台；当前结果支持PDC作为更可靠的基础校正方法，同时也凸显社区校正器在特定场景（如散射光）下的潜力。

Abstract: The correction of systematic errors in TESS light curves is crucial for all astrophysical analyses employing these observations. Here we present a data analysis and a software package, SysCoCoPy, to investigate and directly compare the performance of the Presearch Data Conditioning (PDC) correcting method from the Science Processing Operations Center (SPOC) pipeline and three correctors developed by the community. We incorporate these three correctors based on their implementations in Lightkurve and are particularly interested in the ability of the four correctors to remove scattered light contamination from the Earth and the Moon, which is a key systematic for TESS. We implemented these correctors in SysCoCoPy with a framework that allows an automatic optimization of their parameters to scale the analysis towards increasingly larger samples. SysCoCoPy provides qualitative and quantitative products for the comparison of individual cases as well as statistical results for selected samples. We currently find that while our automatic parameter optimization provides a significant number of successful scattered-light corrections for two of the correctors with a design that favors this purpose, an statistical analysis of our largest sample indicates that PDC is presently more robust, with a larger overall success level of the metrics used.

</details>


### [123] [hyprfine: simulating the 21-cm signal from the Dark Ages through to the Epoch of Reionization on a GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01865)
*Harry T. J. Bevins*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: hyprfine 是一个使用 JAX 和 Python 编写的、原生支持 GPU 的解析型宇宙 21-cm 信号模拟工具，用于快速、可微分地计算红移 z=1100–6 区间内的全天平均信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高效、可微分地模拟宇宙黎明至再电离时期的全天平均 21-cm 信号，以支持参数反演与贝叶斯推断等任务。

Method: 基于解析模型构建 sky-averaged 21-cm 信号演化，利用 JAX 实现 GPU 加速与自动微分，覆盖 Λ-CDM 宇宙学参数和第一代恒星/星系的天体物理过程。

Result: 成为首个原生 GPU 支持、亚秒级运行、跨 GPU 高效并行且在暗时代（z ≥ 35）完全可微分的解析型 21-cm 模拟代码。

Conclusion: hyprfine 为高维参数空间快速采样与梯度优化提供了新工具，显著提升了 21-cm 前沿观测的数据分析能力。

Abstract: hyprfine is an analytic simulation of the sky-averaged 21-cm signal from $z=1100 - 6$ written using JAX and Python for native GPU capabilities. It models the average temperature of the 21-cm signal over cosmic time as a function of the $Λ$-CDM cosmology parameters and the astrophysics of the first stars and galaxies. As far as we are aware, the code is the first analytic GPU native simulation of the 21-cm signal. It runs in a fraction of a second, parallelises efficiently across a GPU and is differentiable through the Dark Ages ($z \geq 35$).

</details>


### [124] [Radio Interferometric Calibration with the Exponential Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01895)
*L. Cabral,M. Fernandez-Corazza,G. Gancio,P. Benaglia*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指数映射和李群的快速最大似然校准估计算法，性能达到Cramér-Rao下界，且比EM算法快约40倍，并成功应用于AS 209原行星盘的实际观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射电干涉仪信号路径中存在乘性和加性扰动，需通过校准加以修正；现有方法在精度与效率之间难以兼顾。

Method: 提出一种基于指数映射和李群的新最大似然估计器，利用微分几何工具建模扰动参数空间。

Result: 所提估计器达到Cramér-Rao下界，运行速度约为EM算法的40倍，并在SMA对AS 209的实测数据中验证有效。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证统计最优性的同时显著提升计算效率，为实时或大规模射电干涉校准提供了新途径。

Abstract: The antenna-elements that make up a radio interferometer form a spatial filter that samples components of the Fourier transform of a target radio astronomical source brightness. Along the signal path, there are multiplicative and additive perturbation effects that alter the signal and that should be corrected. The process of mitigating these perturbation effects is called calibration. In this work, we develop a new and fast Maximum Likelihood based estimator of these perturbation effects using the Exponential Map and Lie groups. To evaluate its performance, we compare it to the Cramér-Rao Lower Bound and, to test the estimation time, we compare it with the Expectation Maximization algorithm, another fast Maximum Likelihood estimator. Finally, we apply our estimator to a real observation of the protoplanetary disk AS 209 made with the Submillimeter Array. We found that our proposed estimator meets the Cramér-Rao Lower Bound and it was approximately 40 times faster than the Expectation Maximization algorithm.

</details>


### [125] [Spurious sources in high-resolution VLA surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02028)
*Eric F. Jiménez-Andrade,Emmanuel Momjian,Eric J. Murphy,James J. Condon,Miguel Vega*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了高分辨率射电连续谱巡天中虚假源过多的问题，发现其主要源于uv采样不完整导致的脏束旁瓣引起的噪声相关性，而非成像方法本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲式河外射电连续谱巡天在亚角秒分辨率下产生了远超高斯噪声预期的虚假源，需探究其成因。

Method: 利用VLA在10GHz对GOODS-N天区的观测数据，分析噪声分布、独立分辨元数量、主波束效应及噪声自相关函数，特别是脏束旁瓣的影响。

Result: 虚假源增多部分源于更多独立分辨元，主波束效应贡献较小；但主要原因是稀疏uv覆盖导致的脏束旁瓣引发的噪声相关性，显著提高虚假探测概率。

Conclusion: 虚假源过剩是uv采样不完整的固有结果，未来深度射电巡天需兼顾高角分辨率与足够致密的uv覆盖以抑制虚假源。

Abstract: Blind extragalactic radio continuum surveys are increasingly conducted at sub-arcsecond angular resolutions. However, these surveys contain substantially more spurious sources than expected from purely Gaussian noise statistics. We investigate the origin of these false detections using the 10GHz GOODS-N mosaic obtained with the Karl G. Jansky Very Large Array (VLA), which reaches a native angular resolution of 0.22arcsec and exhibits a nearly Gaussian pixel-noise distribution. We show that part of the increase in the spurious-source fraction at high angular resolution arises naturally from the larger number of independent resolution elements, while residual wide-field imaging effects associated with the primary-beam response make only a modest contribution. Nevertheless, even after accounting for these effects, the observed fraction of false detections remains several times higher than predicted for Gaussian noise. An analysis of the noise autocorrelation function reveals that images with relatively sparse uv coverage retain significant dirty-beam sidelobes that introduce correlated noise fluctuations, substantially increasing the probability of false detections. We conclude that the excess of spurious sources is an intrinsic consequence of incomplete uv sampling rather than the imaging methodology itself, highlighting that future deep radio continuum surveys require not only high angular resolution but also sufficiently dense uv coverage to minimize spurious detections.

</details>


### [126] [Bayesian Image Reconstruction with Spatially Variant PSFs in X-ray Astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02062)
*Vincent Eberle,Matteo Guardiani,Margret Westerkamp,Philipp Arras,Philipp Frank,Julia Stadler,Torsten Enßlin*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息场论（IFT）的贝叶斯反卷积方法，用于校正X射线望远镜（如Chandra）数据中空间变化的点扩散函数（PSF）和散粒噪声，显著提升图像分辨率与不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线观测数据受空间变化PSF和散粒噪声影响严重，传统方法（如Richardson-Lucy）缺乏对不确定性的自然刻画，且难以处理复杂PSF；需一种物理驱动、可量化不确定性的稳健反演框架。

Method: 结合信息场论（IFT）构建贝叶斯推断模型，采用插值patch-based方法高效表征空间变化PSF；在合成数据上与三种RL算法对比，在模拟和真实Chandra数据（Cas A）上验证性能。

Result: IFT方法在合成数据上SSIM/RMSE/NLL均优于所有RL变体；patch卷积准确建模Chandra复杂PSF；应用于Cas A时图像更锐利、残差符合纯噪声假设，并与高分辨参考数据一致。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效去除空间变化PSF并提供原理性不确定性估计，为多仪器交叉定标和联合分析奠定基础。

Abstract: X-ray observatories introduce unwanted instrumental effects into photon count data that must be accurately accounted for. In particular, the spatially variant point spread function (PSF) and shot noise pose a non-trivial inverse problem. We apply Bayesian inference grounded in information field theory (IFT) combined with a fast spatially variant PSF representation to infer the posterior mean and uncertainty of the X-ray flux under a minimal set of physically motivated prior assumptions, thereby removing or reducing these instrumental effects. First, ignoring spatial PSF variability, the IFT-based deconvolution is benchmarked on a synthetic example against three variants of the Richardson-Lucy (RL) algorithm using SSIM, RMSE, and NLL as evaluation metrics. Second, the ability of an interpolated patch-based convolution scheme to accurately represent the complex, spatially variant Chandra PSF is assessed on simulated PSF data. Finally, deblurring and spatially variant PSF representation are combined and applied to real Chandra observations of the supernova remnant Cassiopeia A. The IFT-based deconvolution outperforms all tested RL variants on the synthetic benchmark. The patch-based convolution scheme accurately recovers the complex structure of the Chandra PSF. Applied to Cassiopeia A, the reconstructed image appears visually sharper than the exposure-corrected data, and the data residuals are consistent with pure noise, indicating an accurate model of both the instrument and the sky. A comparison with a highly resolved reference dataset confirms this impression. The presented framework enables the removal of spatially variant PSFs from noisy data and opens avenues for future work on cross-calibration between multiple observations or different X-ray instruments, building on the principled uncertainty quantification inherent to the Bayesian framework.

</details>


### [127] [A comprehensive simulation framework for multi-modal kilonova observations from all-sky surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02088)
*Felipe Fontinele Nunes,Andrew Toivonen,Farhana Taiyebah,Leonard Lupin-Jimenez,Skylar Callis,Malhar Kulkarni,Soumi De,Michael W. Coughlin*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为kilonova-multimodal-emulator的模拟管道，用于生成包含测光、光谱和图像的多模态千新星观测数据集，以支持人工智能模型在实时巡天数据中高效识别千新星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有巡天项目（如LSST和ZTF）每天产生海量瞬变源候选体，其中千新星因稀有且信号微弱，难以在实时警报流中识别，亟需高效AI模型；而训练此类模型受限于真实千新星观测数据极度匮乏。

Method: 构建了一个名为kilonova-multimodal-emulator的仿真管道，基于BTS的历史观测策略（采样频率与极限星等）和最新辐射转移千新星理论模型，生成面向ZTF类型的、含测光、光谱与图像的逼真多模态模拟数据集。

Result: 成功实现了面向ZTF观测条件的多模态千新星模拟数据生成流程，并产出可用于大规模AI模型训练的综合性合成数据集。

Conclusion: 该模拟管道填补了千新星多模态训练数据的空白，为未来时域巡天中AI驱动的千新星实时识别提供了关键数据基础。

Abstract: Current all-sky surveys such as Vera Rubin's Legacy Survey of Space and Time and the Zwicky Transient Facility (ZTF) promise a wealth of scientific gains that are contained in the millions of astrophysical transient candidates produced each night. Kilonovae, one such transient of interest, will be challenging to identify in the alert stream and will require efficient artificial intelligence models to parse the large, real-time influx of data. In order to build these large models, comprehensive multimodal datasets are necessary for training. Due to a lack of numerous kilonova observations, we propose $\texttt{kilonova-multimodal-emulator}$ -- a simulation pipeline for realistic, multimodal kilonova observations comprised of photometry, spectra, and images. We demonstrate this pipeline for ZTF-type observations, based on historical cadence and limiting magnitude information from the Bright Transient Survey (BTS) and using the latest radiative transfer kilonova models to produce a comprehensive dataset meant for the training of large artificial intelligence model.

</details>
